TASK [sonic_fabric : fabric | Resolve interface metadata from hardware files] ***********************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Border_Leaf1 -> localhost] =>
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        python3 - <<'PY'
        import json
        import os
        import sys

        with open(os.environ["PORT_CONFIG_PATH"], encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            port_rows = {}
            for raw_line in handle:
                line = raw_line.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                fields = line.split()
                if len(fields) < 5:
                    continue
                port_rows[fields[0]] = {
                    "lanes": fields[1],
                    "alias": fields[2],
                    "index": fields[3],
                    "hardware_default_speed": str(fields[4]),
                }

        with open(os.environ["HWSKU_JSON_PATH"], encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            hwsku_data = json.load(handle)

        breakout_cfg = json.loads(os.environ.get("BREAKOUT_CFG", "{}"))

        breakout_by_port = {
            port: values.get("default_brkout_mode", "")
            for port, values in (hwsku_data.get("interfaces") or {}).items()
        }

        resolved = []
        missing = []
        for link in json.loads(os.environ["FABRIC_LINKS"]):
            port = link["port"]
            metadata = port_rows.get(port)
            if metadata is None:
                missing.append(port)
                continue

            merged = dict(link)
            requested_breakout_mode = (breakout_cfg.get(port) or {}).get("brkout_mode", "")

            merged["alias"] = merged.get("alias") or metadata["alias"]
            merged["lanes"] = merged.get("lanes") or metadata["lanes"]
            merged["index"] = merged.get("index") or metadata["index"]
            merged["hardware_default_speed"] = metadata["hardware_default_speed"]
            merged["hardware_default_brkout_mode"] = breakout_by_port.get(port, "")
            if requested_breakout_mode:
                merged["target_breakout_mode"] = requested_breakout_mode
            if not merged.get("speed"):
                merged["speed"] = metadata["hardware_default_speed"]
            resolved.append(merged)

        if missing:
            print(
                "Missing ports in local port_config.ini: " + ", ".join(missing),
                file=sys.stderr,
            )
            sys.exit(1)

        print(json.dumps(resolved))
        PY
    delta: '0:00:00.049471'

TASK [sonic_fabric : apply | Wait for SSH port 22 to recover after interface startup] ***************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Border_Leaf1 -> localhost] =>
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        for i in $(seq 1 24); do
          output=$(ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null           -o ConnectTimeout=5 -o BatchMode=yes -o LogLevel=ERROR           admin@172.16.2.31 echo ok 2>&1)
          rc=$?
          if [ $rc -eq 0 ] || echo "$output" | grep -qi "permission denied"; then
            echo "PORT_OPEN"
            exit 0
          fi
          sleep 5
        done

TASK [sonic_fabric : apply | Wait for SSH port 22 to recover after interface startup] ***************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Border_Leaf2 -> localhost] =>
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        for i in $(seq 1 24); do
          output=$(ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null           -o ConnectTimeout=5 -o BatchMode=yes -o LogLevel=ERROR           admin@172.16.2.32 echo ok 2>&1)
          rc=$?
          if [ $rc -eq 0 ] || echo "$output" | grep -qi "permission denied"; then

changed: [Border_Leaf1] =>
    attempts: 1
    changed: true
    cmd: |-
        # Remove any existing BGP instance running under a DIFFERENT ASN.
        old_asn=$(docker exec bgp vtysh -c 'show running-config' 2>/dev/null          | awk '/^router bgp/{print $3; exit}')
        if [ -n "$old_asn" ] && [ "$old_asn" != "65021" ]; then
          docker exec bgp vtysh    -c "configure terminal"    -c "no router bgp $old_asn"    -c "end"
        fi

        # Apply BGP config with unnumbered (spine-leaf), IP-based (exit), and downlink (server) peers.
        docker exec bgp vtysh  -c "configure terminal"  -c "router bgp 65021"  -c "bgp router-id 10.0.2.1"  -c "no bgp default ipv4-unicast"  -c "no bgp ebgp-requires-policy"  -c "neighbor Ethernet120 interface v6only"  -c "neighbor Ethernet120 remote-as external"  -c "neighbor Ethernet120 description Spine_S2"  -c "neighbor Ethernet120 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet124 interface v6only"  -c "neighbor Ethernet124 remote-as external"  -c "neighbor Ethernet124 description Spine_S1"  -c "neighbor Ethernet124 bfd"  -c "neighbor 10.0.253.1 remote-as 65253"  -c "neighbor 10.0.253.1 description Exit_Router1"  -c "neighbor 10.0.253.1 disable-connected-check"  -c "neighbor 10.0.253.1 bfd"  -c "address-family ipv4 unicast"  -c "redistribute connected"  -c "neighbor Ethernet120 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet124 activate"  -c "neighbor 10.0.253.1 activate"  -c "exit-address-family"  -c "address-family ipv6 unicast"  -c "redistribute connected"  -c "neighbor Ethernet120 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet124 activate"  -c "exit-address-family"  -c "end"  -c "write"

TASK [sonic_fabric : verify | Ping peer IPv6 link-local via fabric interface] ***********************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Border_Leaf1] => (item=Ethernet124 → Spine_S1) =>
    ansible_loop_var: item
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        peer_ll=""
        port="Ethernet124"
        neighbor="Spine_S1"
        if [ -z "$peer_ll" ]; then
          echo "SKIP: peer link-local not yet known for ${port} → ${neighbor} (BGP not Established yet)"
          exit 0
        fi
        if ping6 -c 3 -W 2 -I "$port" "$peer_ll" > /tmp/ping6_${port}.out 2>&1; then
          echo "OK: ping6 ${peer_ll}%${port} → ${neighbor}: 3/3 replies received"
        else
          rtt=$(grep -oP '(?<=time=)\S+' /tmp/ping6_${port}.out | tail -1 || true)
          loss=$(grep -oP '\d+(?=% packet loss)' /tmp/ping6_${port}.out | tail -1 || true)
          echo "FAIL: ping6 ${peer_ll}%${port} → ${neighbor}: ${loss:-100}% packet loss"
          cat /tmp/ping6_${port}.out
        fi
    delta: '0:00:00.005176'
    end: '2026-05-29 23:01:00.569851'
